Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the spectrum object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 712. The moduli space has dimension at most 451 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by spectrum generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 712, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 451. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
